[Editor]
Disable brush coloring.
== 禁用画笔着色。

[Editor]
Disallow/allow unknown or invalid commands.
== 禁用/允许未知或无效命令。

[Editor]
Do not perform quad alignment to other quads/points when moving quads.
== 移动四边形时不与其他四边形/点进行对齐。

[Editor]
Do not preview the paths of quads with a position envelope when a quad layer is selected.
== 选中四边形图层时，不预览带位置动画的四边形路径。

[Editor]
Do not run 'hot_reload' on the local server while rcon authed on map save.
== 保存地图时（RCON 已认证）不在本地服务器执行“hot_reload”。

[Editor]
Do not select layers when ctrl+right clicking on a tile.
== Ctrl+右键图块时不选择图层。

[Editor]
Do not show how weapons, shields, hearts and flags appear ingame.
== 不显示武器、护盾、爱心和旗帜在游戏中的表现。

[Editor]
Do not show quad bounds when moving quads.
== 移动四边形时不显示边界。

[Editor]
Do not show tile information.
== 不显示图块编号.

Done
== 完成

[Editor]
DOOR: Combined with LASER LENGTH creates doors. Doesn't allow to go through it (only with NINJA).
== 门: 与激光长度结合创建门.不允许通过(只有忍者可以).

[Editor]
Double click to create a new point. Use shift to change the zoom axis. Press S to scale selected envelope points.
== 双击创建新点。按住 Shift 切换缩放轴，按 S 缩放选中的动画点。

[Editor]
DRAGGING LASER: Grabs and attracts the closest tee to it. Can reach tees through walls but not through LASER BLOCKER. Medium strength.
== 拖拽激光: 抓取并吸引最近的tee.可以穿过墙壁但不能穿过激光阻挡器到达tee.中等强度.

[Editor]
DRAGGING LASER: Grabs and attracts the closest tee to it. Can reach tees through walls but not through LASER BLOCKER. Strong.
== 拖拽激光: 抓取并吸引最近的tee.可以穿过墙壁但不能穿过激光阻挡器到达tee.强.

[Editor]

== 拖拽激光: 抓取并吸引最近的tee.无法穿过墙壁和激光阻挡器到达tee.中等强度.

[Editor]

== 拖拽激光: 抓取并吸引最近的tee.无法穿过墙壁和激光阻挡器到达tee.强.

[Editor]
DRAGGING LASER: Grabs and attracts the closest tee to it. Can't reach tees through walls and LASER BLOCKER. Weak.
== 拖拽激光: 抓取并吸引最近的tee.无法穿过墙壁和激光阻挡器到达tee.弱.

[Editor]
Draw after lightly smoothing the mouse path.
== 轻量平滑鼠标路径后绘制。

[Editor]
Drawing tool: Drag a rectangle to fill it with the current tile. An empty brush erases to air.
== 绘图工具：拖拽矩形区域，用当前图块填充。空画笔会擦除为空气。

[Editor]
Drawing tool: Drag to create a rectangle, ellipse, triangle or N-gon.
== 绘图工具：拖拽生成矩形、椭圆、三角形或 N 边形。

[Editor]
Drawing tool: Draw a continuous line along the mouse path.
== 绘图工具：沿鼠标路径绘制连续线条。

Drawing tool: Probabilistically scatter tiles with a fade inside the dragged rectangle.

Envelope
== 动画

[Editor]
Envelope point drag
== 拖动动画点

[Editor]
Envelope point. Left mouse to drag. Hold ctrl to be more precise. Hold shift to alter time. Shift+right click to delete.
== 动画点：左键拖动，按住 Ctrl 精细调整，按住 Shift 改变时间，Shift+右键删除。

[Editor]
EXPLODING BULLET: Bounces off the walls with explosion. Touching the bullet works like FREEZE tile (freezes for 3 seconds by default).
== 爆炸子弹: 从墙壁弹开时会爆炸.接触子弹像冻结图块一样工作(默认冻结3秒).

[Editor]
GLOBAL OLD SHOTGUN: Shotgun drags others always towards the shooter, even after having bounced. Shooter can't hit themselves. Place only one tile somewhere on the map.
== 全局旧激光: 启用旧激光模式. 激光不能击中自己, 霰弹枪会将其他目标始终拉向玩家, 即使目标已经弹开也不例外. 仅可在地图上的任意位置放置一块图块.

Hookthrough
== 穿钩

[Editor]
HOOKTHROUGH: Combined with (UN)HOOKABLE tiles, allows to hook through the walls.
== 钩穿: 与(不)可钩图块结合,允许钩穿墙壁.

[Editor]
HOOKTHROUGH: Combined with a collision tile is new hookthrough, otherwise stops hook, from one direction.
== 钩穿: 与碰撞图块结合是新钩穿,否则从一个方向阻止钩子.

[Editor]
HOOKTHROUGH: Combined with a collision tile is new hookthrough, otherwise stops hooks, from all directions.
== 钩穿: 与碰撞图块结合是新钩穿,否则从所有方向阻止钩子.

[Editor]
HOOKTHROUGH: Shortcut for new hookthrough.
== 钩穿: 新钩穿的快捷方式.

A render command failed. Try to update your GPU drivers.

[Graphics error]
An error during command recording occurred. Try to update your GPU drivers.
== 指令记录期间检测到错误。请尝试更新显卡驱动程序。

[Graphics error]
Could not initialize the given graphics backend, reverting to the default backend now.
== 无法初始化当前配置的图形后端，正在将配置回退至默认图形后端。

[Graphics error]
Could not initialize the given graphics backend, this is probably because you didn't install the driver of the integrated graphics card.
== 无法初始化当前配置的图形后端，请检查此电脑是否已安装集成显卡的驱动程序。

[Graphics error]
Failed during initialization. Try to change gfx_backend to OpenGL or Vulkan in qmclient/settings.cfg in the config directory and try again.
== 初始化失败。请尝试打开配置目录中的设置文件（qmclient/settings.cfg）并将 “gfx_backend” 设置为 “gfx_backend OpenGL” 或 “gfx_backend Vulkan” 再重试。

[Graphics error]
Failed to swap framebuffers. Try to update your GPU drivers.
== 帧缓存交替失败。请尝试更新显卡驱动程序。

[Graphics error]
Out of VRAM. Try setting 'cl_skins_loaded_max' to a lower value or remove custom assets (skins, entities, etc.), especially those with high resolution.
== 显存不足。请尝试将 'cl_skins_loaded_max' 设置为较低的值或移除自定义资源（皮肤、实体等），尤其是高分辨率的资源。

[Graphics error]
Submitting the render commands failed. Try to update your GPU drivers.
== 渲染指令提交失败。请尝试更新显卡驱动程序。

[Graphics error]
Unknown error. Try to change gfx_backend to OpenGL or Vulkan in qmclient/settings.cfg in the config directory and try again.
== 未知错误。请尝试打开配置目录中的设置文件（qmclient/settings.cfg）并将 “gfx_backend” 设置为 “gfx_backend OpenGL” 或 “gfx_backend Vulkan” 再重试。
